Seven feature sheets name the client directories of `layout`, `chat` and `settings` (WP-10N), so each gains a line for `account` and for `workspace` (R18). An `optional` line carries no range, which corrects the line of the registrants. The four client tests of the moved drawings mount their element alone, because loading the chat's built part from `terminal` would close a cycle of packages.
- Version 4 (2026-11-17). After the third review of this spec, a Scope review of what changed since version 2 (2 Major, 4 Nit, no Blocker), and the owner's decision 30, which answered both Major findings.
  - **S1, FIX, by the owner's decision 30 (U27).** Four contracts change the meaning of a call and take a new id: `layout.tabs/v2`, `layout.panel/v3`, `chat.hooks/v2` and `runtime/v2`. Decision 27 now covers `runtime.lease/v1` alone (U23), and `core.open-settings/v1` stays as decided (U21), so the six versioning deviations are two. Contracts and versions states the four new majors once, with every consumer and the change that moves it. D30 adds each new id beside the old one and removes the old one inside the same step: step 4(a) goes from 9 merged changes to 13, step 5(c) from 1 to 6, and phase 7 gets the steps 7(a) to 7(c) with 5 merged changes. Under the new ids the fields that had lost their effect are gone: `icon` of a tab request, and `terminals` of the runtime's snapshot. `transport` now changes, so 24 bundles change. No platform version and no `engines.doric` moves for it. U14 is reworded: decision 30 changes the part of OD23 that kept the ids `layout.tabs/v1` and `layout.panel/v2`. New in Risks: R17, and in R15 a custom composition that requires `runtime/v1`.
  - **S2, FIX, by the owner's decision 30 (U28).** A bundle test that phase 1 skips because the product is wrong, or because it passes and fails on one commit, gets its own Linear issue, which the skip names, and is fixed outside this spec. Section 2, R6, AC1.3 and Non-goals say so, and AC13.4 asks that every remaining skip names an open issue.
  - **S3, FIX.** The lock stays, and the test of a concurrent writer and the race clause of R5 are gone: the host is stopped, and only the startup check reads the tables.
  - **S4, FIX.** `settings.models/v1` publishes no array of efforts. Its fields take the runtime's type through a type-only import, and `settings` keeps its own array unexported.
  - **S5, FIX.** The host README gets one sentence beside its link to the composition file, naming the lines a custom composition adds, and no list of bundles.
  - **S6, FIX.** Every merged change that completes a step or a phase adds an Evolution entry, besides those that carry release notes.
  - **Numbers that changed.** The criteria of phase 7 are renumbered in step order (the five of version 3 are AC7.3 to AC7.7), its two tests swap numbers, and the last test of phase 10 takes the number of the one that left.
- Version 3, extended the same day (2026-11-17). The owner's decision 29 answered the three questions of version 3, each as proposed. No design decision changed, so the version number stays; the concept's Evolution extended its version 3 the same way.
  - **Q3, now U24.** The three platform minors that raise `engines.doric` are accepted, each with the release note "update the desktop app before the host". R14 stays and cites U24.
  - **Q4, now U25.** The image builds through the Nx target, and the owner reopened the reason recorded at `app/backend/doric-host/.Dockerfile:27-30`. U8 names it as the one recorded decision that is reopened, and Terms says that it is not a kept decision.
  - **Q5, now U26.** `site` is added as designed. The basis is that the owner knows no consumer outside the repository that validates a kind with a closed schema.
  - **Also.** [Questions](#questions) holds none. Alternatives gains the two options of Q4 and Q5 it did not have: the image keeping its two hand-written lists, and step 6(b) waiting for the known consumers. D26 names step 5(a) where it named phase 5. The documentation plan is checked against the steps of this version (its version 3): its inventory row of `docs/architecture/bundles/versioning.md` names the six versioning deviations where it said "Nothing found", it places the compatibility rule in phase 0 and the `AGENTS.md` of the two new bundles in steps 4(b) and 4(c), and its agents read `main` after the last code phase in merge order.

Phases 2, 5 and 11 get lettered steps, and the Phases table gives the number of merged changes of each step. Assigning them showed a gap: between steps 4(a) and 4(d) nothing opened or reconciled the Thread tabs. The layout's navigation keeps doing both until step 4(c), where the chat takes over, and the chat defines the tab's icon in step 4(a). The criteria and tests of phases 2, 4, 5, 6, 10 and 11 are renumbered in step order.

  - **CO1, FIX.** Terms and Acceptance criteria define the phase report: who writes it, where it is and who checks it. AC13.5 asks for commands and their output in place of "the review".
  - **CO2, FIX.** No reason cites a working note. D12, D18, D28, section 4, section 9 and section 17 give the evidence itself.

Section 16 says what a build from before phase 10 does against the migrated database, on the evidence of Prisma's documentation and of the code, and AC10.5 runs it before the phase merges (R16).
  - **CR2, FIX.** R14 names the risk, section 18 says what an older app shows, and the release notes of each change that raises a range say to update the app first. Version 2 also raised `engines.doric` in step 6(b) for the core, the four providers and `settings`; that was wrong by the repository's own rule, and step 6(b) now raises none, so an older app does not lose the core. The cost of D29 is stated in Alternatives. Q3 asks the owner.

The finding called the failure silent; that holds for `account`, while without `workspace` the dependent bundles are quarantined and named.
  - **CR4, FIX.** `default` puts no deadline on a source's `read`. `mcp` bounds its own read at 30,000 ms today and keeps it, so no read that succeeds today fails afterwards.

For consumers outside the repository the rule and the announcement move to phase 0, R8 no longer offers a revert as the mitigation, and Q5 asks the owner.

- Version 2 (2026-11-17). After the Scope review of version 1 (4 Major, 5 Nit, no Blocker) and the owner's decision 27, which answered both questions of version 1: Q1, where the quarantined-bundle notice sits in the dashboard, and Q2, the version of a contract that keeps its id and loses a member. The helpers move to `tests/support/workspace.mjs`, the existing test becomes the `imports` test of `tests/boundaries.test.mjs`, and `npm run lint:test` runs both files; no script and no CI step is added (D23, section 1).

The chat writes no grouping or effort rule (D13, section 11). The contract gains an argument and a `reload`, which the concept's "one read and one subscription" did not have; the read's failure and its Retry need them. The shared molecule becomes a platform export by the one rule of D29.

  - **Nit 8, FIX.** The chat's tab icon uses the chat's own `isAgentThread` and no copy of `thread-kind` (section 10).
  - **Nit 9, FIX.** The `recorded` list and its test are gone. The kept decisions are stated in the concept's Decisions, confirmed in the Evolution entries of the phases that touch them, and listed and checked in phase 13 (AC13.5).

- Version 1 (2026-11-17). First version, written from the accepted concept (version 5), the owner's decisions 1–26 and the code at commit `a868b5d0`. Decision 26 (OD26, U21) arrived while this version was written: `core.open-settings/v1` keeps its id and takes a minor as a stated exception to the versioning table, which takes that contract out of D2 and leaves Q2 open for five contracts. It settles the two conflicts the concept left to the spec: X19, by the order of the sidebar column and Q1; and X21, by the Nx tag pattern. Where the code contradicted the concept, this spec says so in place: the history buttons (section 9), the model picker (section 11), the runtime's fourth use of `terminal/v1` (section 12), the tests that name a slot (section 8), the number of bundles that change (header) and the platform version of step 6(b) (section 18).
